- First frame (`KF_CAVG1_S01_START`): `assets/frames/KF_CAVG1_S01_START.jpg`

```text
1.2m ground-level 24mm Anamorphic extreme wide shot, slow push-in across churning harbor waves. Godzilla is a 110-meter 90000-ton colossal amphibious titan kaiju with rugged charcoal-black crocodilian armored scales, a towering upright muscular torso, thick pillar-like legs, a jagged row of sharp maple-leaf dorsal plates running down its spine that radiate Cherenkov-blue atomic light when charged, and a massive long muscular whip-tail (current state/wardrobe: wet charcoal-black crocodilian scales cascading with foaming ocean water, unlit bone-grey jagged dorsal fins, ferocious roaring jaws). Choreography timeline: [0.0s-2.5s Entry State & Telegraph] Camera: 1.2m ground-level 24mm Anamorphic extreme wide shot, slow push-in across churning harbor waves. Action: Godzilla swims submerged beneath churning black bay swells on the right side of the harbor, jagged dorsal plates slicing toward the shattered seawall. VFX/Env: Rainy night in ruined city, dark teal and orange lighting, volumetric fog in ENV_ACT1_HARBOR_RUINS. SFX: deafening thunderclap, torrential rain lashing wet asphalt, deep sub-bass underwater rumble, crashing ocean waves. [2.5s-5.5s Primary Biomechanical & Mechanical Action] Camera: 1.2m ground-level 24mm Anamorphic extreme wide shot, slow push-in across churning harbor waves. Action: Godzilla surges through the storm-lashed harbor water on the right, displacing a massive wall of white sea foam across the flooded coastal road. VFX/Env: IMAX 70mm cinematic VFX, heavy slow-motion weight, realistic water and debris displacement. SFX: deafening thunderclap, torrential rain lashing wet asphalt, deep sub-bass underwater rumble, crashing ocean waves. [5.5s-8.0s Consequence & Exit State Lock] Camera: 1.2m ground-level 24mm Anamorphic extreme wide shot, slow push-in across churning harbor waves. Action: Godzilla's jagged dorsal fins tower above the boiling harbor surface on the right, facing left toward the flooded city ruins. VFX/Env: Settling floodwater waves, sparks, and steam matching exit state. SFX: deafening thunderclap, torrential rain lashing wet asphalt, deep sub-bass underwater rumble, crashing ocean waves. A flooded, ruined modern coastal city harbor at night during a heavy thunderstorm, where deep ocean bay water crashes over a shattered seawall onto flooded coastal streets with burning skyscrapers in the background, IMAX 70mm film look. IMAX 70mm theatrical blockbuster VFX cinematography, rainy night in ruined coastal city, dark moody teal and orange Hollywood color grading, volumetric fog, wet asphalt reflecting firelight and city lights, realistic heavy slow-motion weight, 180-degree shutter natural motion blur. Ambient sound only: deafening thunderclap, torrential rain lashing wet asphalt, deep sub-bass underwater rumble, crashing ocean waves. No subtitles, no on-screen text.
```

ChernoAlpha is an 85-meter 2400-ton brutalist heavy mechanical Jaeger robot with weathered olive-drab industrial steel armor, a massive cylindrical nuclear-reactor tower rising from its chest in place of a head, an amber-lit recessed conning-tower cockpit visor on its sternum, twin oversized shoulder flame-turbine exhaust pods, and colossal hydraulic piston-driven siege fists (current state/wardrobe: intact weathered olive-drab industrial steel armor drenched in heavy rain, amber sternum cockpit visor lit, hydraulic arm pistons primed).
